================================================================================
TELEGRAM CHAT TRANSCRIPT - YEAR 2020
================================================================================
Chat            : GSMG Puzzle Solvers
Chat type / id  : private_supergroup / 1166734859
This file covers: 2020-01-01 through 2020-12-31
Messages here   : 2990 (2692 chat messages + 298 service events) of 61349 in the full export
First / last    : 2020-01-01 08:14:32  ->  2020-12-31 22:01:24
Series          : one file per year, 2019, 2020, 2021, 2022, 2023, 2024, 2025, 2026 (this file is 2020)
Source          : result.json (Telegram Desktop JSON export)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
HOW TO READ THIS FILE
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Days begin with:      ## DAY YYYY-MM-DD (Weekday)
Each message block:   [#<id>] HH:MM:SS <Display Name> (<user id>)
                      (annotation lines, zero or more, in parentheses)
                      <message text, may span several lines>
Blocks are separated by a blank line. Times are HH:MM:SS under the DAY
date above them, in the timezone the export was taken in.

#<id> is the Telegram message id. It is unique across the whole export and
increases with time, so replies can be traced across files by id.

Annotation lines you may see:
  (reply to #N - Author: "quoted snippet")   this message answers #N;
     a [YYYY-MM-DD] after the id means #N is from another day/file
  (forwarded from X) / (sent via bot @b) / (edited YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS)
  (attachment: ...)  media was posted; the binary is NOT part of this export
  (poll: "question" ...)                     a poll and its vote counts
  (reactions: EMOJI xN, ...)                 emoji reactions on the message
Lines starting with  * SERVICE  are membership events, not chat messages.

Author names are normalised: a given user id always appears under the same
display name (the one they used most often), so a person stays recognisable
even where the raw export recorded no name for a message.
Inline `code` and fenced ``` blocks preserve the original code formatting.

[#2353] 16:38:26 Legik (user646609236)
What does this nonsense mean?

here's a guy who theorised the idea that 'Any linear electrical network with voltage and current sources and only resistances can be replaced at terminals A–B by an equivalent current source Ino in parallel connection with an equivalent resistance Rno'. He might have been insecure. His competition might have been that as well. However, after enough belikins this competition tried to become a ruler of a piece of land that's technically the poorest of the entire planet. 4 rulers have shared the first name of the competition. 2 had the firstname in the surname. One of the rulers had a number, and dirty too. Another had a resemblance to Carrey, James Gates, also Simulacra and Simulation. Another ruler was at some point a floating zerg house while being under control of the dirty one.. too. The one after died too soon. Moral: never execute an order that revokes the highest power or you might suddenly get killed. The 5binary code is a part of the piece of this puzzle.
